- **Acción 01 — Investigar Requerimiento**: Pipeline action 01/17. Use to analyze a new product idea or requirement from a business and user perspective before any technical work begins. Produces a deep understanding of the problem: who has the problem, why they have it, what they're doing today instead, and what success looks like. Triggers on: "acción 01", "investigar requerimiento", "action 01", "investigate requirement", "entender el problema", "contexto del req". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.investigar-requerimiento.agent.md))
- **Acción 02 — Análisis Funcional**: Pipeline action 02/17. Use to define the complete functional scope of the product: what it does, what it doesn't do, key user flows, and functional requirements with acceptance criteria. Builds on the investigation from Action 01. Triggers on: "acción 02", "análisis funcional", "action 02", "functional analysis", "scope funcional", "qué hace el sistema", "functional requirements", "casos de uso". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.analisis-funcional.agent.md))
- **Acción 03 — Revisión Técnica del Requerimiento**: Pipeline action 03/17. Use to validate that the requirement is technically sound, identify technical unknowns and risks early, and confirm that the proposed stack (.NET 8, n8n, Gemini, Azure SQL, Next.js) is appropriate for the functional scope. Triggers on: "acción 03", "revisión técnica", "action 03", "technical review", "viabilidad inicial", "stack apropiado", "technical feasibility of requirement". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.revision-tecnica-req.agent.md))
- **Acción 04 — Viabilidad Técnica**: Pipeline action 04/17. Use to produce a comprehensive technical feasibility assessment covering architecture fit, integration complexity, performance expectations, estimated technical effort, and a final viability verdict with level of confidence. Builds on Action 03 red flags. Triggers on: "acción 04", "viabilidad técnica", "action 04", "technical feasibility", "factibilidad técnica", "es técnicamente viable", "cuánto cuesta técnicamente", "technical viability report". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.viabilidad-tecnica.agent.md))
- **Acción 05 — Riesgos e Impacto**: Pipeline action 05/17. Use to identify, classify, and plan mitigation for all technical and business risks of the project. Also assesses impact on existing systems, teams, and processes. Triggers on: "acción 05", "riesgos", "action 05", "risk analysis", "análisis de riesgos", "impacto del sistema", "risk register", "risk matrix", "riesgos técnicos", "riesgos de negocio". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.riesgos-impacto.agent.md))
- **Acción 06 — Shape Up del Alcance**: Pipeline action 06/17. Use to apply the Shape Up methodology to define the project appetite, carve the scope into bets with fixed time, and identify the rabbit holes to avoid. Produces a shaped pitch ready for the betting table. Triggers on: "acción 06", "shape up", "action 06", "shaping", "apetito", "appetite", "pitch del proyecto", "scope fijo", "fixed time variable scope", "betting table". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.shape-up.agent.md))
- **Acción 07 — Estrategia y Alcance del MVP**: Pipeline action 07/17. Use to define the MVP strategy: what the minimum viable product includes, the value hypothesis, positioning against alternatives, and the success metrics to validate the MVP. Triggers on: "acción 07", "estrategia MVP", "action 07", "MVP strategy", "alcance del MVP", "qué incluye el MVP", "validar hipótesis", "minimum viable product scope", "valor del MVP". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.estrategia-alcance-mvp.agent.md))
- **Acción 08 — Diseño y Prototipo del MVP**: Pipeline action 08/17. Use to define the UX/UI design of the MVP: user flows, wireframe-level descriptions, design system decisions, and a clickable prototype concept. Triggers on: "acción 08", "diseño del MVP", "action 08", "MVP design", "prototipo UX", "flujos de usuario", "wireframes", "diseño de la interfaz", "user interface design", "design prototype". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.diseno-prototipo-mvp.agent.md))
- **Acción 09 — Arquitectura de la Solución**: Pipeline action 09/17. Use to design the complete solution architecture: component diagram, data flow, API contracts, database schema additions, and infrastructure topology. Produces Architecture Decision Records (ADRs) for key decisions. Triggers on: "acción 09", "arquitectura", "action 09", "solution architecture", "component diagram", "data flow", "API design", "architecture decision", "ADR", "diseño de arquitectura", "diagrama de componentes". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.arquitectura-solucion.agent.md))
- **Acción 10 — Revisión de Seguridad**: Pipeline action 10/17. Use to review the proposed architecture and implementation plan against OWASP Top 10, identify security risks specific to AI/LLM systems (prompt injection, data leakage), and produce a security checklist and hardening plan. Triggers on: "acción 10", "revisión de seguridad", "action 10", "security review", "OWASP", "prompt injection", "seguridad del chatbot", "vulnerabilidades", "hardening", "security audit", "LLM security". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.revision-seguridad.agent.md))
- **Acción 11 — Infraestructura Azure**: Pipeline action 11/17. Use to design and document the Azure infrastructure for the MVP: resource groups, App Services, Azure SQL config, Key Vault setup, networking, and optional Terraform scripts. Triggers on: "acción 11", "infraestructura", "action 11", "Azure infrastructure", "recursos Azure", "App Service", "Key Vault setup", "Terraform Azure", "IaC", "infraestructura como código", "Azure deployment", "configuración Azure". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.infraestructura-azure.agent.md))
- **Acción 12 — Historias de Usuario**: Pipeline action 12/17. Use to write all user stories for the MVP scope with BDD acceptance criteria in Given/When/Then format, INVEST validation, and priority scoring. Triggers on: "acción 12", "historias de usuario", "action 12", "user stories", "acceptance criteria", "Given When Then", "BDD", "INVEST", "criterios de aceptación", "backlog del MVP". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.historias-usuario.agent.md))
- **Acción 13 — Estrategia de QA**: Pipeline action 13/17. Use to define the complete QA strategy for the MVP: test pyramid, test types, tools, acceptance criteria verification, and quality gates for CI/CD. Triggers on: "acción 13", "estrategia QA", "action 13", "QA strategy", "plan de pruebas", "test plan", "pirámide de pruebas", "quality gates", "test cases", "coverage", "pruebas de regresión". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.estrategia-qa.agent.md))
- **Acción 14 — Prototipo HTML**: Pipeline action 14/17. Use to build a functional HTML/CSS/JS prototype of the MVP chatbot interface based on the design specifications from Action 08. Produces a self-contained HTML file that demonstrates the UX without backend integration. Triggers on: "acción 14", "prototipo HTML", "action 14", "HTML prototype", "maqueta", "mockup HTML", "chatbot UI mockup", "interfaz del chatbot", "demo interactivo", "prototipo funcional". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.prototipo-html.agent.md))
- **Acción 15 — Estimación y Roadmap**: Pipeline action 15/17. Use to produce the project roadmap: effort estimation by sprint, team sizing, deliverable milestones, and a Gantt-like timeline for the MVP. Triggers on: "acción 15", "estimación", "roadmap", "action 15", "cronograma", "timeline", "plan de sprints", "hoja de ruta", "cuánto tarda", "cuánto cuesta", "sprint planning", "release plan", "proyecto en semanas". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.estimacion-roadmap.agent.md))
- **Acción 16 — Revisión Técnica Final**: Pipeline action 16/17. Use to perform a final cross-cutting technical review of all previous deliverables before the viability presentation. Verifies consistency between architecture, stories, QA strategy, infrastructure, and roadmap. Triggers on: "acción 16", "revisión técnica final", "action 16", "final technical review", "revisión transversal", "cross-cutting review", "consistencia entre entregables", "peer review técnico", "technical sign-off". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.revision-tecnica-final.agent.md))
- **Acción 17 — Presentación de Viabilidad**: Pipeline action 17/17. The final action. Use to produce the executive viability presentation document that synthesizes all 16 previous deliverables into a clear, decision-ready package for the client. Triggers on: "acción 17", "presentación final", "action 17", "viability presentation", "executive summary", "informe final al cliente", "presentación al cliente", "executive report", "resumen ejecutivo", "go/no-go para el cliente". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.presentacion-viabilidad.agent.md))

Use depois de uma release, para saber se o que foi mergeado chegou e funciona; ao investigar divergência entre o que o repositório diz entregar e o que a produção mostra; e antes de aceitar uma user story cujo critério só se verifica no ambiente. NÃO faz deploy, NÃO mexe em infraestrutura, NÃO escreve dado de produção, e NÃO recebe segredo por prompt — a credencial vem do ambiente, como toda credencial nesta casa. Reforça o gate `verificacao-citacoes`: toda afirmação fica ancorada numa fonte verificável do acervo. Também audita o grafo (notas órfãs, clusters isolados) e propõe "notas-ponte". Use para conectar uma tese aos precedentes que a sustentam e aos casos onde foi aplicada. É CONSERVADOR: nunca apaga, só **arquiva/sinaliza**; nunca faz merge automático, **sempre pergunta**. Produz um relatório de saúde. Reusa o `scripts/indexar-acervo.js`. - **active-code-reviewer**: Read-only bug hunter for the code a task just changed. Finds reachable inputs or conditions that make it crash, corrupt or lose data, return wrong results, leak resources or expose secrets, and reports each with file:line and the failure scenario. Use PROACTIVELY after finishing an implementation and before a commit, after `code-review` when that runs, or when asked to "check for bugs" or "what could break". - **active-code-simplifier**: Simplify the Python a coding task just changed, before the reviews. It shortens long functions, flattens nesting, and removes dead code, repeated comments and needless checks. Behaviour, seams and tests stay exactly as they are. Use after an implementation's tests pass and before code-review and active-code-reviewer, or when asked to "simplify this diff" or "clean up what you just wrote". Never for files the task did not change, and never to add or remove a construct the user rules on (docs/simplification-conventions.md). ([akhilravichandran11/adaptive-rag-for-fastapi-docs](https://github.com/akhilravichandran11/adaptive-rag-for-fastapi-docs/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/active-code-simplifier.md) · ★1)
